HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000849/B001","root_001651/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1744797447c0a4387b5b","text":"1. **Counterfeit speech within the person.** Evidence: و س و س B001, hidden inward whisper; ق و ل B001/B012 from 114:1, uttered speech and unspoken inner saying; ص د ر B001, chest as bodily front. Structural cue: the first ayah commands outward saying, while this ayah names inward whispering. Assigned role: وسوس is a counterfeit qawl that remains inside rather than becoming accountable utterance. Abductive move: because the remedy is “say,” the disease is an alternative form of saying. Reading change: the ayah reads as an invasion of speech-function, not merely as a temptation located somewhere inside.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000849/B002","root_001651/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2297df6f5085f44929b3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:5:2\",\"root_001651\",[\"B001\"]],[\"114:5:4\",\"root_000849\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Göğsün başlangıç ve ön kısım anlamı, fısıltının söz ve davranış daha oluşurken en erken aşamada etkide bulunduğunu gösterir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_004965/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2385ce075f4565b542a8","text":"3. **Human recipients can become a social relay.** The fixed `ٱلنَّاسِ` activates ن و س `B001` wavering motion, while ص د ر `B004` makes the breasts potential issuing points. Contextual ق و ل `B007` supplies talk circulating among people, and 114:6 places `ٱلنَّاسِ` among the whispering sources after naming them here as recipients. The packet supplies those two human roles; I infer reception -> outward circulation -> renewed reception. The ayah then reads as one stage in a human transmission network, not only as an isolated private temptation.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001651/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_281131b85f5d6ce0b1ed","text":"4. **The concealed source can be hard to separate from its inner effect.** Immediately after `صُدُورِ`, 114:6 supplies ج ن ن `B001` (concealment, including keeping something hidden in the breast) and `B010` (the hidden heart, inward fear, or concealed inner matter). Those images overlap spatially with و س و س `B001` in the fixed ayah. The packet supplies the overlap; I infer concealment of source within the site of effect -> attributional ambiguity. `يُوَسْوِسُ فِى صُدُورِ` can therefore preserve both an agentive whisperer and an inward agitation whose origin is experientially obscured, without collapsing them into one reading.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3b33d25707e4015d1d24","text":"114:6 produced two linked surprises: ج ن ن `B001/B010` placed concealment and hidden inner matter in the same breast-space, and final `ٱلنَّاسِ` turned the recipient noun into a source noun. Together they exposed both source/effect ambiguity and the possibility of a recipient-to-transmitter relay.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000849/B001","root_001651/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_41c74db8a4de8d93dced","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:5:2\",\"root_001651\",[\"B002\"]],[\"114:5:4\",\"root_000849\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Belli belirsiz fısıltı, bedensel göğüs boşluğunda hissedilen hafif bir iç kıpırtı gibi duygusal ve bedensel bir boyut da kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000849/B004","root_001651/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5bbf0cdeaaaf97599675","text":"1. **The breast is a conversion chamber, not merely a container.** و س و س `B001` supplies the hidden inward prompt, and ص د ر `B001` supplies the bodily chest; the preposition `فِى` explicitly locates the act there. Yet ص د ر `B004` also images a source from which actions or forms issue. The packet supplies entry/location and issuing-source; I infer an inward prompt processed there -> outward conduct or speech. The fixed phrase consequently reads as a mechanism by which concealed input can become apparently self-originating output.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000849/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_71a3219d6d53fa5ea96e","text":"114:6 changed the force of “صدور الناس” by showing that the source is not restricted to an invisible outside. The final “من الجنة والناس” made the chest a junction where concealed sources and human sources meet. Earlier ayat also returned: “قل” reappeared as the exposed/public alternative to وسوس, and “ملك الناس” reappeared through م ل ك B005 as control of the mainstay/source. The surprise is that “chests” are not passive containers; with ص د ر B002/B004 they are front, beginning, and source, the place where hidden sound becomes human motion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_004965/B001","root_004965/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_85ada0a72df5019d3f1d","text":"4. **The swaying population as the resonant medium.** Evidence: ن و س B001, swaying or dangling oscillation; ن و س B002, driving camels; م ل ك B008 from 114:2, leading forepart in animals; خ ن س B005 from 114:4, veering horse. Structural cue: الناس appears again after the chest-location, making the interior plural and repeated. Assigned role: people are a resonating medium that can sway, be driven, or veer under the hidden impulse. Abductive move: the same inner whisper becomes collective motion because each chest belongs to the repeated الناس. Reading change: the ayah reads as a social-embodied mechanism: inner whispers can move a population.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000849/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_8d810809f56ccefdeec7","text":"2. **Whispering acts upstream, at the beginning of action.** ص د ر `B002` means the front, foremost, or first part of a thing, including the beginning of speech or an affair. This dormant temporal image is activated by the contrast between `يُوَسْوِسُ` here and the commanded overt `قُلْ` in 114:1. The packet supplies hidden speech in the “first part” and later articulation; I infer intervention at inception -> influence over what is eventually said or done. `فِى صُدُورِ` therefore marks an upstream point of leverage rather than only anatomical depth.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000849/B001","root_000849/B002","root_000849/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_97e6dda9d9dc10b20262","text":"2. **Chest as source and first point of action.** Evidence: ص د ر B001, chest; ص د ر B002, foremost or first part; ص د ر B004, source from which forms issue; م ل ك B005 from 114:2, mainstay of a matter; ر ب ب B002 from 114:1, repair/nurture/completion. Structural cue: the phrase “في صدور الناس” follows the authority triad over الناس. Assigned role: the chest is not only a container but the front/source from which action and orientation issue. Abductive move: the whisperer chooses the chest because controlling the source controls what later emerges as action, belief, or public speech. Reading change: “in the breasts” reads as at the command-origin of the human being.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001651/B001","root_001651/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_9aff933b93f51ec19f57","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:5:2\",\"root_001651\",[\"B001\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"İç fısıltı, açıkça söylenen sözün yerine kişinin kendi düşüncesiymiş gibi işleyen sahte bir söz kanalı kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000849/B001","root_001651/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9bb3a2dc185665722f7c","text":"3. **Subtle physical murmur in an embodied interior.** Evidence: و س و س B002, hidden rustle or murmur; ص د ر B001, anatomical chest and what covers or marks it; ج ن ن B016 from 114:6, chest bones; ج ن ن B010, hidden heart/inner fear. Structural cue: the packet moves from named divine authority to an embodied location. Assigned role: the whisper has tactile/acoustic faintness within the ribbed bodily space. Abductive move: the lexicon’s faint sounds allow the threat to be imagined as barely perceptible movement inside the body. Reading change: the ayah becomes somatic and affective, not only cognitive.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000849/B001","root_001651/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_d4e92366c8b15f0c99b5","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:5:2\",\"root_001651\",[\"B001\"]],[\"114:5:4\",\"root_000849\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kaynak etkisini göğsün içinde gizlediği için, dışarıdan gelen bir telkinle kişinin iç sıkıntısını ayırmak deneyim bakımından güçleşebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000849/B001","root_000849/B004","root_001651/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_e34257679a828b02ce91","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"114:5:2\",\"root_001651\",[\"B001\"]],[\"114:5:4\",\"root_000849\",[\"B001\",\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Göğüs yalnız bir kap değil, gizli telkinin davranışa veya söze dönüşerek dışarı çıkabildiği bir kaynak noktasıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
